```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-001
  source_function_2: XY
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-001
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Canonical source identity is document/version scoped; repeated source is not independent corroboration; authority
    classes have different scopes.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
    corrected_statement_or_field: Keep the canonical statement, but classify duplicate-source non-independence and authority-scope rules as internal
      provenance boundaries supported by source examples, not as external market relations.
    corrected_classification: CM-MKT-CR-001_AND_CR-002 -> RECLASSIFY_AS_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_WITH_SOURCE_BACKED_EXAMPLES
    boundary_note: Canonical identity requires document/rule/page, version or effective state, evidence location, and organization; URL alias
      or local ID alone is insufficient.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XZ
    correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split RU-001 into two typed positions. (A) INTERNAL_SOURCE_IDENTITY_AND_INDEPENDENCE_RULE: repeated appearance
      of one canonical document/version is one source identity group and is not independent corroboration. (B) SOURCE_SCOPED_AUTHORITY_POSITION:
      a controlling rule, final rule, operating page, FAQ, research explanation, alert, enforcement record or future guidance may support only
      the question, mechanism and effective interval bound to that exact source.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_AND_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE
  - source_function_2: YZ
    correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split the unit into two typed surfaces. SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION: controlling rules, final rules, operating pages,
      FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective guidance have distinct authority and effective-state scopes. INTERNAL_EVIDENCE_PROCESSING_BOUNDARY:
      canonical-source deduplication and the rule that repetition is not independent corroboration are processing rules, not external market relations.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_BACKED_SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION_AND_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY
  application_decision: SPLIT_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE_FROM_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY
  applied_statement_or_field: Exact controlling rules, final rules, operating pages, FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective
    guidance retain their own source-backed authority and effective-state fields. Canonical source identity is document/version/evidence-location
    scoped. Deduplication and the rule that repeated appearance is not independent corroboration are internal evidence-processing boundaries,
    not external market relations.
  applied_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_EVIDENCE_AND_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY
```

```yaml
      institution-specific definitions remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED rather than universally source-backed.

      NASDAQ-05 is PRIMARY_HISTORICAL_CURRENT_STATUS_UNCONFIRMED; SEC-03 is HISTORICAL_WITH_LATER_AMENDMENTS. Business Date and Session Date remain
      institution-specific missing counterparts rather than universal clock types.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_EXPLICIT_CURRENT_FUTURE_HISTORICAL_SPLIT
  application_decision: APPLY_EFFECTIVE_STATE_AND_EXPLICIT_MISSING_COUNTERPART_BOUNDARY
  applied_statement_or_field: Publication, Effective, Retrieval, Revision, Market Event, Report, Calendar Date and Trade Date are distinct clock/date
    fields. Future-effective and historical materials are not current operation. At retrieval 2026-07-25, the Rule 605 first-firm-uncrossed-quote
    guidance is FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_2026-08-01; historical Nasdaq and SEC records retain historical/amended status. Session Date and Business Date
    remain institution-scoped MISSING_COUNTERPARTS where no canonical definition is bound. Scheduled time is not actual event time.
```

#### `XY-MKT-F2-CORR-009`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-009
  source_function_2: XY
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-009
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Multiple price objects are non-equivalent; finality is split across six axes and one final boolean is prohibited.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: Source-backed examples establish that auction execution, continuous trade, component open, live index, SOQ,
      daily settlement and final settlement can be different objects and lifecycle states. The identity rule 'same numeric value is not automatically
      the same object' is an internal object-identity boundary. The six finality fields and prohibition of one boolean are a schema candidate,
      not an external market fact.
    corrected_classification: CR-015_SPLIT_SOURCE_EXAMPLES_PLUS_INTERNAL_IDENTITY_BOUNDARY; CR-016_RECLASSIFY_AS_SCHEMA_CANDIDATE
    boundary_note: Daily settlement may be final for a daily publication while not being expiration Final Settlement.
  peer_positions:

    or formation-mechanism identity. Event, auction, publication, trade-date, settlement and contract finality are stored as an internal multi-axis
```

```yaml
correction_application:

  source_function_2: XY
```

```yaml
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Component open, live opening index, SOQ input/finalization and final settlement are distinct; prior close or last
    sale fallback is preserved.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Component official open, evolving live opening index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and futures final settlement
      remain distinct. State the fallbacks by stage: the live opening index may use prior close for a component not yet open; SOQ uses the source-defined
      last-sale fallback only when a component does not open that day.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_STAGE_SPECIFIC_FALLBACKS
    boundary_note: Prior close and last sale are not interchangeable generic fallbacks.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XZ
    correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split stage-specific component handling. LIVE_OPENING_INDEX: a constituent not yet open may retain the prior
      close while the live opening sequence evolves. SOQ_INPUT: use the constituent official opening price when it opens. SOQ_NO_OPEN_FALLBACK:
      use the source-defined last-sale fallback only if the constituent does not open that day. FINAL_FUTURES_SETTLEMENT: apply the finalized
      SOQ to the expiring contract.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_PRICE_OBJECT_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  - source_function_2: YZ

      final settlement. Apply only within the currently recorded CME methodology boundary; exact index-provider version, weights and constituent
      opening-state reconstruction remain missing.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_METHODOLOGY_VERSION_AND_COMPONENT_DATA_GAP
  application_decision: APPLY_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  applied_statement_or_field: Component Official Open, evolving Live Opening Index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and Futures Final Settlement are
    stage-specific objects. A live index may retain prior close for a not-yet-open component; SOQ uses official opening input when available and
    the source-defined last-sale fallback only when the component does not open that day; finalized SOQ is then applied to the expiring futures
    contract.
  applied_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  preserved_source_refs:

  - CME-05
```

```yaml
      state_exit, reopen mechanism and effective rule version for every record. Retain the Function.1 correction that NYSE LULD is a cash-equity

      guidance cannot source CME mechanics. Current LULD and venue reopen rule text remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.'
```

#### `XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
  source_function_2: XZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-001
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Source Identity는 URL 문자열이나 Function 내부 Local ID가 아니라 Canonical Document·Rule·Operating Page·FAQ·Historical
    Record·Effective Version의 결속으로 판정한다. 동일 Canonical Source가 X/Y/Z 또는 여러 Function.1에 반복되어도 Independent Corroboration으로 재계산하지
    않는다. Controlling Rule, Final Rule, Operating Page, FAQ/Glossary, Research Explanation, Historical Alert와 Future-effective
    Guidance는 Authority Scope와 Effective State가 다르다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split RU-001 into two typed positions. (A) INTERNAL_SOURCE_IDENTITY_AND_INDEPENDENCE_RULE:
      repeated appearance of one canonical document/version is one source identity group and is not independent corroboration.
      (B) SOURCE_SCOPED_AUTHORITY_POSITION: a controlling rule, final rule, operating page, FAQ, research explanation, alert,
      enforcement record or future guidance may support only the question, mechanism and effective interval bound to that
      exact source.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_AND_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE
    boundary_note: Canonical URL alone is insufficient; document/rule number, title, version, publication/effective time and
      evidence location remain part of identity. Generic authority ordering is not a universal external market fact.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
    corrected_statement_or_field: Keep the canonical statement, but classify duplicate-source non-independence and authority-scope

      and effective-state scopes. INTERNAL_EVIDENCE_PROCESSING_BOUNDARY: canonical-source deduplication and the rule that
      repetition is not independent corroboration are processing rules, not external market relations.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_BACKED_SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION_AND_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY
  application_decision: SPLIT_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE_FROM_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY

    identity is document/version/evidence-location scoped. Deduplication and the rule that repeated appearance is not independent
```

#### `XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-002`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-002
  source_function_2: XZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-002
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Publication, Effective, Retrieval, Market Event, Report, Revision, Calendar Date, Trade Date,
    Session Date와 Business Date는 독립 Clock/Date fields다. ET·CT·UTC와 DST는 Date-bound normalization을 요구한다. Scheduled Time은 Actual
    Event Time이 아니며 Current-effective는 Future-effective 또는 Historical-effective와 다르다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the independent clock/date fields. Treat Publication, Effective, Retrieval, Market
      Event, Report, Revision, Calendar Date and Trade Date as source-backed scoped distinctions. Treat Session Date and Business
      Date as required schema fields whose institution-specific definitions remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED rather than universally
      source-backed.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_TEMPORAL_BOUNDARY_WITH_SCHEMA_GAPS
    boundary_note: ET/CT/UTC conversion requires the event date and DST rule. Scheduled time is a rule boundary, not evidence
      that a symbol-level event occurred.

    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-002

    corrected_statement_or_field: Publication, effective, retrieval, revision, market-event, report, calendar and trade-date
      fields are distinct. Session Date and Business Date remain institution-scoped candidate fields and MISSING_COUNTERPART
      where no canonical institution-specific definition is bound. Scheduled time is not actual event time; future-effective
      and historical material must not be represented as current operation.

      Business Date and Session Date remain institution-specific missing counterparts rather than universal clock types.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_EXPLICIT_CURRENT_FUTURE_HISTORICAL_SPLIT

  applied_statement_or_field: Publication, Effective, Retrieval, Revision, Market Event, Report, Calendar Date and Trade Date
    are distinct clock/date fields. Future-effective and historical materials are not current operation. At retrieval 2026-07-25,
    the Rule 605 first-firm-uncrossed-quote guidance is FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_2026-08-01; historical Nasdaq and SEC records retain
    historical/amended status. Session Date and Business Date remain institution-scoped MISSING_COUNTERPARTS where no canonical
```

```yaml
      reliance on SEC S08 enforcement as the general source. Bind the formal state to the recorded Rule 605 sources SEC-02/SEC-03
      with future-effective state, or retain MISSING_FORMAL_SOURCE_BINDING until that binding is materialized.
```

#### `XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-009`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-009
  source_function_2: XZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-009
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Auction Execution Price, Continuous Trade Price, Component Opening Price, Live Index Value,
    SOQ, Daily Settlement와 Final Settlement는 서로 다른 Price Objects다. Finality는 event_final, auction_final, publication_final,
    trade_date_final, settlement_final과 contract_final로 분리하며 하나의 Boolean `final`을 금지한다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: RECLASSIFY_AS_SCHEMA_CANDIDATE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Keep object-specific source-backed examples for auction execution, continuous trade, component
      open, live index, SOQ, daily settlement and final settlement. Classify event_final, auction_final, publication_final,
      trade_date_final, settlement_final and contract_final as an internal multi-axis schema candidate. Do not classify the
      schema itself as a source-backed market relation.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_PRICE_OBJECT_EXAMPLES_PLUS_INTERNAL_FINALITY_SCHEMA
    boundary_note: CM-MKT-CR-015 must split source-backed examples from the object-identity rule. CM-MKT-CR-016 must be reclassified
      as a schema candidate.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-009
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: Source-backed examples establish that auction execution, continuous trade, component open,
      live index, SOQ, daily settlement and final settlement can be different objects and lifecycle states. The identity rule
      'same numeric value is not automatically the same object' is an internal object-identity boundary. The six finality
      fields and prohibition of one boolean are a schema candidate, not an external market fact.
    corrected_classification: CR-015_SPLIT_SOURCE_EXAMPLES_PLUS_INTERNAL_IDENTITY_BOUNDARY; CR-016_RECLASSIFY_AS_SCHEMA_CANDIDATE
  - source_function_2: YZ
    correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-009
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'SOURCE_BACKED CLAUSE: auction execution, continuous trade, component open, live index,
      SOQ, daily settlement and final settlement are non-equivalent price objects. SCHEMA CLAUSE: event_final, auction_final,
      publication_final, trade_date_final, settlement_final and contract_final are an internal multi-axis schema candidate.
      The prohibition on one Boolean `final` is an internal scope boundary.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_PRICE_OBJECT_RELATIONS_PLUS_INTERNAL_MULTI_AXIS_FINALITY_SCHEMA
  application_decision: SPLIT_SOURCE_PRICE_OBJECT_RELATION_INTERNAL_IDENTITY_BOUNDARY_AND_SCHEMA
  applied_statement_or_field: Auction execution, continuous trade, component official open, live index value, SOQ, daily settlement
    and final settlement are distinct source-defined price objects and lifecycle states. Numeric equality does not establish
    object, official-authority or formation-mechanism identity. Event, auction, publication, trade-date, settlement and contract
    finality are stored as an internal multi-axis schema candidate; one Boolean final is prohibited.
  applied_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_PRICE_OBJECT_RELATIONS_PLUS_INTERNAL_IDENTITY_AND_FINALITY_SCHEMA
```

#### `XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
  source_function_2: XZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-011
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Component Official Opening Price, Live Opening Index Value, SOQ Input, SOQ Finalization와 Futures
    Final Settlement는 Methodology로 연결되지만 동일 Object/Event가 아니다. Component가 열리지 않을 때 Prior Close 또는 Last Sale 등 Source-defined
    Fallback을 보존한다. SOQ는 ordinary one-timestamp cash open이 아니다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split stage-specific component handling. LIVE_OPENING_INDEX: a constituent not yet open
      may retain the prior close while the live opening sequence evolves. SOQ_INPUT: use the constituent official opening
      price when it opens. SOQ_NO_OPEN_FALLBACK: use the source-defined last-sale fallback only if the constituent does not
      open that day. FINAL_FUTURES_SETTLEMENT: apply the finalized SOQ to the expiring contract.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_PRICE_OBJECT_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
    boundary_note: Prior close and last sale are not interchangeable fallback labels; their eligibility depends on the methodology
      stage and condition.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Component official open, evolving live opening index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and futures
      final settlement remain distinct. State the fallbacks by stage: the live opening index may use prior close for a component
      not yet open; SOQ uses the source-defined last-sale fallback only when a component does not open that day.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_STAGE_SPECIFIC_FALLBACKS
  - source_function_2: YZ
    correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the distinction among component official opens, live opening index, SOQ inputs/finalization
      and futures final settlement. Apply only within the currently recorded CME methodology boundary; exact index-provider
      version, weights and constituent opening-state reconstruction remain missing.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_METHODOLOGY_VERSION_AND_COMPONENT_DATA_GAP
  application_decision: APPLY_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  applied_statement_or_field: Component Official Open, evolving Live Opening Index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and Futures
    Final Settlement are stage-specific objects. A live index may retain prior close for a not-yet-open component; SOQ uses
    official opening input when available and the source-defined last-sale fallback only when the component does not open
    that day; finalized SOQ is then applied to the expiring futures contract.
  applied_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  preserved_source_refs:
  - SRC-G003
  - CME-05
  - Z:S01/S04
  - CME-01/02/03/05/06
  - Source Equivalence and Independence Map
  - Bound Y source IDs and Z source refs
  preserved_conflict:
  - same components and morning do not create object identity
  preserved_counterexample:
  - a constituent can use prior close in the live index and later contribute an official open to SOQ
  - a never-opened constituent can trigger last-sale fallback
  exclusion: null
  unresolved:
  - specific expiration reconstruction
  - current index-provider methodology/version
  next_data_required:
  - official S&P DJI SOQ file and methodology version
  - constituent-level opening-state dataset
  application_state: APPLIED
```

#### `XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
  source_function_2: XZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-012
  original_Result_XYZ_position: CME Scheduled Halt, CME Price-limit State/Halt, NMS LULD Limit State, LULD Pause, Regulatory
    Halt, Reopen Auction와 Remainder-day Termination은 Trigger·Authority·Entry·Exit가 다른 States다. Limit State에서 Trading이 경계가격으로
    계속될 수 있으므로 Limit State는 Halt가 아니다. Cash LULD를 Futures Halt로 이식하지 않는다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the typed halt/limit taxonomy. Require market, venue, authority, trigger, state_entry,
      execution_permission, state_exit, reopen mechanism and effective rule version for every record. Retain the Function.1
      correction that NYSE LULD is a cash-equity LULD state, not FUTURES_HALT_STATE.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STATE_TAXONOMY_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_NEXT_DATA
    boundary_note: A limit state may permit trading at the boundary and is not automatically a halt; a halt is not automatically
      a full-day closure.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    review_verdict: CORRECT_SOURCE_BINDING
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the taxonomy but split source binding by market and authority. CME limit/halt claims
      require CME sources; NMS LULD limit/pause/reopen claims require the current LULD Plan and venue rule; regulatory halt
      claims require regulator scope. Y-EVT-03 cannot carry SEC/CME scope from one SEC future-guidance source.
    corrected_classification: PARTIALLY_SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_TEXT_NEXT_DATA
  - source_function_2: YZ
    correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and
      venue reopen auctions by market, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Correct Y-EVT-03''s combined SEC/CME
      organization scope: its bound SEC future guidance cannot source CME mechanics. Current LULD and venue reopen rule text
      remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.'
    corrected_classification: PARTIAL_SOURCE_BACKING_WITH_MARKET_AUTHORITY_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SPLIT
  application_decision: APPLY_MARKET_AUTHORITY_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SOURCE_SPLIT
  applied_statement_or_field: CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and venue reopen
    auctions are split by market, venue, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Limit State is not automatically
    Halt; cash LULD is not futures halt. CME mechanics require CME sources, LULD requires current Plan/venue rules, and one
    SEC future-guidance source cannot authorize CME mechanics. Current LULD and reopen rule text remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.
  applied_classification: PARTIAL_SOURCE_BACKING_WITH_MARKET_AUTHORITY_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SPLIT
```

#### `YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
  source_function_2: YZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-001
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Source Identity는 URL 문자열이나 Function 내부 Local ID가 아니라 Canonical Document·Rule·Operating Page·FAQ·Historical Record·Effective
    Version의 결속으로 판정한다. 동일 Canonical Source가 X/Y/Z 또는 여러 Function.1에 반복되어도 Independent Corroboration으로 재계산하지 않는다. Controlling Rule, Final Rule,
    Operating Page, FAQ/Glossary, Research Explanation, Historical Alert와 Future-effective Guidance는 Authority Scope와 Effective State가 다르다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split the unit into two typed surfaces. SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION: controlling rules, final rules, operating pages,
      FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective guidance have distinct authority and effective-state scopes. INTERNAL_EVIDENCE_PROCESSING_BOUNDARY:
      canonical-source deduplication and the rule that repetition is not independent corroboration are processing rules, not external market relations.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_BACKED_SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION_AND_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY
    boundary_note: Authority ranking is question- and mechanism-scoped; it is not one universal hierarchy.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
    corrected_statement_or_field: Keep the canonical statement, but classify duplicate-source non-independence and authority-scope rules as internal
      provenance boundaries supported by source examples, not as external market relations.
    corrected_classification: CM-MKT-CR-001_AND_CR-002 -> RECLASSIFY_AS_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_WITH_SOURCE_BACKED_EXAMPLES
  - source_function_2: XZ
    correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split RU-001 into two typed positions. (A) INTERNAL_SOURCE_IDENTITY_AND_INDEPENDENCE_RULE: repeated appearance
      of one canonical document/version is one source identity group and is not independent corroboration. (B) SOURCE_SCOPED_AUTHORITY_POSITION:
      a controlling rule, final rule, operating page, FAQ, research explanation, alert, enforcement record or future guidance may support only
      the question, mechanism and effective interval bound to that exact source.'
    corrected_classification: MIXED_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_AND_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE
  application_decision: SPLIT_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE_FROM_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY
  applied_statement_or_field: Exact controlling rules, final rules, operating pages, FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective
    guidance retain their own source-backed authority and effective-state fields. Canonical source identity is document/version/evidence-location
    scoped. Deduplication and the rule that repeated appearance is not independent corroboration are internal evidence-processing boundaries,
    not external market relations.
  applied_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_EVIDENCE_AND_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY
  preserved_source_refs:
  - SRC-G004
  - SRC-G005
  - SRC-G006
  - SRC-G007
  - SRC-G013
  - X:SEC-04
  - X:SEC-05
  - SRC-G008
  - SRC-G011
  - SRC-G010
  - source equivalence map
  - Source Equivalence and Independence Map
  - Bound Y source IDs and Z source refs

  preserved_counterexample: The same canonical Nasdaq or CME document can recur across multiple inputs without creating a second independent source.
  exclusion: Do not classify CR-001 in its current form as a source-backed market relation.
```

```yaml
      institution-specific missing counterparts rather than universal clock types.
```

#### `YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-006`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-006
  source_function_2: YZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-006
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Official Open은 하나의 Universal Type이 아니다. NASDAQ_NORMAL_CROSS_NOOP, NASDAQ_NO_CROSS_FALLBACK_NOOP, NYSE_AUCTION_OFFICIAL_OPEN,
    RTH_SESSION_BOUNDARY, FIRST_FIRM_UNCROSSED_QUOTE_BENCHMARK를 분리한다. Normal Cross와 No-cross Fallback은 같은 NOOP field를 만들 수 있지만 Formation Mechanism과
    Execution Path가 다르다.
```

```yaml
      and final settlement are non-equivalent price objects. SCHEMA CLAUSE: event_final, auction_final, publication_final, trade_date_final, settlement_final
      and contract_final are an internal multi-axis schema candidate. The prohibition on one Boolean `final` is an internal scope boundary.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_PRICE_OBJECT_RELATIONS_PLUS_INTERNAL_MULTI_AXIS_FINALITY_SCHEMA
    boundary_note: Source-backed examples support distinct lifecycle meanings but do not externally standardize this exact six-axis schema.

      the same object' is an internal object-identity boundary. The six finality fields and prohibition of one boolean are a schema candidate,

      contract_final as an internal multi-axis schema candidate. Do not classify the schema itself as a source-backed market relation.
```

#### `YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
  source_function_2: YZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-011
  original_Result_XYZ_position: Component Official Opening Price, Live Opening Index Value, SOQ Input, SOQ Finalization와 Futures Final Settlement는
    Methodology로 연결되지만 동일 Object/Event가 아니다. Component가 열리지 않을 때 Prior Close 또는 Last Sale 등 Source-defined Fallback을 보존한다. SOQ는 ordinary one-timestamp
    cash open이 아니다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the distinction among component official opens, live opening index, SOQ inputs/finalization and futures
      final settlement. Apply only within the currently recorded CME methodology boundary; exact index-provider version, weights and constituent
      opening-state reconstruction remain missing.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_METHODOLOGY_VERSION_AND_COMPONENT_DATA_GAP
    boundary_note: SOQ is an asynchronous component methodology, not one universal 09:30 snapshot.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Component official open, evolving live opening index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and futures final settlement
      remain distinct. State the fallbacks by stage: the live opening index may use prior close for a component not yet open; SOQ uses the source-defined
      last-sale fallback only when a component does not open that day.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_STAGE_SPECIFIC_FALLBACKS
  - source_function_2: XZ
    correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split stage-specific component handling. LIVE_OPENING_INDEX: a constituent not yet open may retain the prior
      close while the live opening sequence evolves. SOQ_INPUT: use the constituent official opening price when it opens. SOQ_NO_OPEN_FALLBACK:
      use the source-defined last-sale fallback only if the constituent does not open that day. FINAL_FUTURES_SETTLEMENT: apply the finalized
      SOQ to the expiring contract.'
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_PRICE_OBJECT_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  application_decision: APPLY_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  applied_statement_or_field: Component Official Open, evolving Live Opening Index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and Futures Final Settlement are
    stage-specific objects. A live index may retain prior close for a not-yet-open component; SOQ uses official opening input when available and
    the source-defined last-sale fallback only when the component does not open that day; finalized SOQ is then applied to the expiring futures
    contract.
  applied_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
  preserved_source_refs:
  - SRC-G003
  - CME-05
  - Z:S01/S04
  - CME-01/02/03/05/06
  - Source Equivalence and Independence Map
  - Bound Y source IDs and Z source refs
  preserved_conflict: Same underlying and same morning do not create object or event identity.
  preserved_counterexample: An unopened component can use prior close in the live index and a later official open in SOQ.
  exclusion: Do not equate SOQ with ordinary opening index value, one stock open or daily settlement.
  unresolved: Exact current S&P DJI methodology/version and selected-expiration constituent data.
  next_data_required: Index-provider methodology, weights, official SOQ file and component opening records.
  application_state: APPLIED_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
```

#### `YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012`

```yaml
correction_application:
  correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
  source_function_2: YZ
  result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-012
  original_Result_XYZ_position: CME Scheduled Halt, CME Price-limit State/Halt, NMS LULD Limit State, LULD Pause, Regulatory Halt, Reopen Auction와
    Remainder-day Termination은 Trigger·Authority·Entry·Exit가 다른 States다. Limit State에서 Trading이 경계가격으로 계속될 수 있으므로 Limit State는 Halt가 아니다. Cash
    LULD를 Futures Halt로 이식하지 않는다.
  source_Function_2_position:
    review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
    corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and venue reopen auctions
      by market, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Correct Y-EVT-03''s combined SEC/CME organization scope: its bound SEC future
      guidance cannot source CME mechanics. Current LULD and venue reopen rule text remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.'
    corrected_classification: PARTIAL_SOURCE_BACKING_WITH_MARKET_AUTHORITY_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SPLIT
    boundary_note: Limit State can permit trading at the boundary and is not automatically a Halt.
  peer_positions:
  - source_function_2: XY
    correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    review_verdict: CORRECT_SOURCE_BINDING
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the taxonomy but split source binding by market and authority. CME limit/halt claims require CME sources;
      NMS LULD limit/pause/reopen claims require the current LULD Plan and venue rule; regulatory halt claims require regulator scope. Y-EVT-03
      cannot carry SEC/CME scope from one SEC future-guidance source.
    corrected_classification: PARTIALLY_SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_TEXT_NEXT_DATA
  - source_function_2: XZ
    correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
    corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the typed halt/limit taxonomy. Require market, venue, authority, trigger, state_entry, execution_permission,
      state_exit, reopen mechanism and effective rule version for every record. Retain the Function.1 correction that NYSE LULD is a cash-equity
      LULD state, not FUTURES_HALT_STATE.
    corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STATE_TAXONOMY_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_NEXT_DATA
  application_decision: APPLY_MARKET_AUTHORITY_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SOURCE_SPLIT
  applied_statement_or_field: CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and venue reopen auctions are split
    by market, venue, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Limit State is not automatically Halt; cash LULD is not futures halt.
```

### `CM-MKT-RD-001` — Source Identity·Authority·Independence

```yaml
record_contract:
  record_id: CM-MKT-RD-001
  source_result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-001
  subject: Source Identity·Authority·Independence
  original_Result_XYZ_statement: Source Identity는 URL 문자열이나 Function 내부 Local ID가 아니라 Canonical Document·Rule·Operating Page·FAQ·Historical Record·Effective
    Version의 결속으로 판정한다. 동일 Canonical Source가 X/Y/Z 또는 여러 Function.1에 반복되어도 Independent Corroboration으로 재계산하지 않는다. Controlling Rule, Final Rule,
    Operating Page, FAQ/Glossary, Research Explanation, Historical Alert와 Future-effective Guidance는 Authority Scope와 Effective State가 다르다.
  applied_Function_2_corrections:
    XY: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    XZ: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
    YZ: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
  preserved_Function_2_positions:
    XY:
      correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-001
      review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
      corrected_statement_or_field: Keep the canonical statement, but classify duplicate-source non-independence and authority-scope rules as
        internal provenance boundaries supported by source examples, not as external market relations.
      corrected_classification: CM-MKT-CR-001_AND_CR-002 -> RECLASSIFY_AS_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_WITH_SOURCE_BACKED_EXAMPLES
    XZ:
      correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
      review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
      corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split RU-001 into two typed positions. (A) INTERNAL_SOURCE_IDENTITY_AND_INDEPENDENCE_RULE: repeated appearance
        of one canonical document/version is one source identity group and is not independent corroboration. (B) SOURCE_SCOPED_AUTHORITY_POSITION:
        a controlling rule, final rule, operating page, FAQ, research explanation, alert, enforcement record or future guidance may support only
        the question, mechanism and effective interval bound to that exact source.'
      corrected_classification: MIXED_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY_AND_SOURCE_SCOPED_EVIDENCE
    YZ:
      correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-001
      review_verdict: SPLIT_COMPOUND_STATEMENT
      corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split the unit into two typed surfaces. SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION: controlling rules, final rules, operating
        pages, FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective guidance have distinct authority and effective-state scopes.
        INTERNAL_EVIDENCE_PROCESSING_BOUNDARY: canonical-source deduplication and the rule that repetition is not independent corroboration are
        processing rules, not external market relations.'
      corrected_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_BACKED_SOURCE_CLASSIFICATION_AND_INTERNAL_SCOPE_BOUNDARY
  canonical_statement: Exact controlling rules, final rules, operating pages, FAQs, research explanations, historical alerts and future-effective
    guidance retain their own source-backed authority and effective-state fields. Canonical source identity is document/version/evidence-location
    scoped. Deduplication and the rule that repeated appearance is not independent corroboration are internal evidence-processing boundaries,
    not external market relations.
  canonical_classification: MIXED_SOURCE_EVIDENCE_AND_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_BOUNDARY
  source_backed_clauses:
  - Exact source classes have distinct authority scopes and effective states within their bound questions and mechanisms.
  internal_boundary_clauses:
  - Canonical source deduplication is an internal provenance rule.
  - Repeated source appearance is not independent corroboration.
  - Generic authority hierarchy is not universal.
  schema_candidate_clauses: []
  source_identity_refs:
  - SRC-G004
  - SRC-G005
  - SRC-G006
  - SRC-G007
  - SRC-G013
  - X:SEC-04
  - X:SEC-05
  - SRC-G008
  - SRC-G011
  - SRC-G010
  - source equivalence map
  - Source Equivalence and Independence Map
  - Bound Y source IDs and Z source refs
  source_independence_state: DEDUPLICATED_BY_CANONICAL_DOCUMENT_VERSION_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE
  source_authority_scope: QUESTION_AND_MECHANISM_SCOPED
  publication_effective_event_retrieval_state:
  - SOURCE_CLASS_AND_VERSION_DEPENDENT
  - EVENT_DATE_REPORT_DATE_RETRIEVAL_DATE_SEPARATED
  - SOURCE_AND_VENUE_SCOPED
  - publication/effective/event/retrieval/revision fields
  - Authority and effective state preserved per source
  - Scoped by institution/rule/effective state
  - NOT_APPLICABLE
  market_venue_instrument_scope:
    market: MULTI
    venue: MULTI
    instrument: MULTI
    contract_or_security: AS_DECLARED
    session: AS_DECLARED
    timezone: AS_DECLARED
  formal_fields:
    source_identity_group: REQUIRED
    source_independence_group: REQUIRED
    source_authority_class: RULE_OR_PAGE_OR_EXPLANATION_OR_HISTORICAL_OR_FUTURE_GUIDANCE
    authority_scope: QUESTION_AND_MECHANISM_SCOPED
    source_effective_state: CURRENT/FUTURE/HISTORICAL_SPLIT
    market_event_time: NOT_APPLICABLE
    market: MULTI
    venue: MULTI
    instrument: MULTI
    formation_mechanism: NOT_APPLICABLE
    official_authority: SOURCE_SCOPED
    failure_or_fallback: SOURCE_LINEAGE_FAILURE_PRESERVED
  compatible_differences:
  - binding rule
  - operational summary
  - parameter display
  - interpretive FAQ
  - dated contingency
  - event evidence
  - operation/event
  - later enforcement report
  - current interpretation
  - Nasdaq vs NYSE open
  - SEC vs CME halt
  - Nasdaq vs NYSE order class
  - same canonical source across X/Z vs independent authority groups
  - rulebook
  - operating page
  - FAQ
  - research explanation
  - final rule
  - staff guidance
  - historical enforcement
  - same word across instruments, venues and mechanisms
  - Y authority/definition gaps
  - Z data/feed/model gaps
  reference_frame_differences: []
  conflicts:
  - FAQ or operating page can be current yet not override controlling rulebook.
  - Several Y organization fields exceed the scope of their single bound source.
  - same source cited as corroboration
  - '`>` is not a universal ranking across different authority scopes.'
  - One Word ≠ One Universal State.
  - Equal missing counts do not imply item identity.
  ambiguities:
  - OFFICIAL_WITHOUT_DOCUMENT_CLASS
  - REPORT_TIME_AS_OCCURRENCE_TIME
  - CROSS_INSTITUTION_LABEL_WITH_SINGLE_SOURCE
  - local source ID
  - Official explanatory evidence can be current but not controlling rule text.
  - '`Official` is authority-scoped, not universal factual truth.'
  - Some gaps overlap partially, such as CFD methodology and consolidated feed data.
  counterexamples:
  - A FAQ can mark a change pending while a current operating page displays the new interval.
  - The 2023 NYSE event was described in a 2026 SEC order.
  - Y-SES-05 names NYSE/CME but its bound source and definition are NYSE-only.
  - SEC enforcement publication years after event
  - A dated FAQ can contain a pending transition while the current operating page reflects a later state.
  - Opening Price can mean component official open, venue official price or index opening value.
  - A formal O11 object does not make a provider methodology available.
  exclusions:
  - Do not average authority classes or treat same institution as same document.
  - Do not date the event by the publication date.
  - Exclude unsupported institution half from Source-backed status; retain it as scoped next-data candidate.
  - unbound source as canonical
  - Do not treat same canonical source repeated in Y and Z as independent corroboration.
  - Do not normalize solely by field name.
  - Do not fill any missing item by inference.
  missing_counterparts: []
  unresolved:
  - complete revision histories for dynamic pages
  - NYSE exact auction-only definitions
  - CME pre-open term canon
  - current SEC/CME halt mappings
  - source version metadata gaps
  - Exact archival rule versions for litigation-grade identity.
  - Project canonical type naming convention.
  - Fifteen semantic missing groups remain.
  next_data_required:
  - archived exact rule/page versions for litigation-grade use
  - institution-specific source for each split candidate
  - append-only source corrections
  - versioned rule archives and amendment lineage
  - scoped type namespace and discriminator registry
  - see Missing Counterpart Alignment Matrix
  validation_boundary: Current exact Function.1 objects and their recorded source/authority/effective-state boundaries only.
```

```yaml
record_contract:
```

```yaml
  subject: Order Lifecycle·Eligibility·Execution
  original_Result_XYZ_statement: Order Entry → Acceptance → Modification/Cancellation → Eligibility → Matchability → Execution 또는 Non-execution의
    Lifecycle을 분리한다. Pre-open Order, Auction-only Interest, Continuous-book Interest와 Cancel/Resubmit branch는 Order objects이며 Trade가 아니다. Matchable
    Candidate도 Cross가 발생하기 전에는 Execution이 아니다.

        remain separate. Replace generic cross-institution 'Auction-only Interest' with venue-scoped auction order types or descriptive categories
        whose controlling rule is separately bound.

        eligibility_state, matchability_state, execution_occurred and failure_or_resubmit_branch fields.
      corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_ORDER_LIFECYCLE_WITH_VENUE_RULE_BOUNDARY
    YZ:

      review_verdict: CORRECT_SOURCE_BINDING

        require independent controlling sources. Accepted, eligible, matchable and executed remain separate states.

    lifecycle states. Venue-specific order types require separately bound controlling sources; generic Auction-only/Auction Interest labels remain
```

```yaml
record_contract:
```

```yaml
    RTH_SESSION_BOUNDARY, FIRST_FIRM_UNCROSSED_QUOTE_BENCHMARK를 분리한다. Normal Cross와 No-cross Fallback은 같은 NOOP field를 만들 수 있지만 Formation Mechanism과
    Execution Path가 다르다.
```

```yaml
        trade_object and official_authority as a schema candidate derived from the evidence boundary, not as an externally mandated market schema.
```

```yaml
        the same object' is an internal object-identity boundary. The six finality fields and prohibition of one boolean are a schema candidate,
```

### `CM-MKT-RD-011` — Component Opens·Opening Index·SOQ·Final Settlement

```yaml
record_contract:
  record_id: CM-MKT-RD-011
  source_result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-011
  subject: Component Opens·Opening Index·SOQ·Final Settlement
  original_Result_XYZ_statement: Component Official Opening Price, Live Opening Index Value, SOQ Input, SOQ Finalization와 Futures Final Settlement는
    Methodology로 연결되지만 동일 Object/Event가 아니다. Component가 열리지 않을 때 Prior Close 또는 Last Sale 등 Source-defined Fallback을 보존한다. SOQ는 ordinary one-timestamp
    cash open이 아니다.
  applied_Function_2_corrections:
    XY: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    XZ: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
    YZ: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
  preserved_Function_2_positions:
    XY:
      correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-011
      review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
      corrected_statement_or_field: 'Component official open, evolving live opening index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and futures final settlement
        remain distinct. State the fallbacks by stage: the live opening index may use prior close for a component not yet open; SOQ uses the source-defined
        last-sale fallback only when a component does not open that day.'
      corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_STAGE_SPECIFIC_FALLBACKS
    XZ:
      correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
      review_verdict: CORRECT_STATEMENT
      corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split stage-specific component handling. LIVE_OPENING_INDEX: a constituent not yet open may retain the prior
        close while the live opening sequence evolves. SOQ_INPUT: use the constituent official opening price when it opens. SOQ_NO_OPEN_FALLBACK:
        use the source-defined last-sale fallback only if the constituent does not open that day. FINAL_FUTURES_SETTLEMENT: apply the finalized
        SOQ to the expiring contract.'
      corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_PRICE_OBJECT_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
    YZ:
      correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-011
      review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
      corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the distinction among component official opens, live opening index, SOQ inputs/finalization and futures
        final settlement. Apply only within the currently recorded CME methodology boundary; exact index-provider version, weights and constituent
        opening-state reconstruction remain missing.
      corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_METHODOLOGY_VERSION_AND_COMPONENT_DATA_GAP
  canonical_statement: Component Official Open, evolving Live Opening Index, SOQ input, SOQ finalization and Futures Final Settlement are stage-specific
    objects. A live index may retain prior close for a not-yet-open component; SOQ uses official opening input when available and the source-defined
    last-sale fallback only when the component does not open that day; finalized SOQ is then applied to the expiring futures contract.
  canonical_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STAGE_SPECIFIC_INDEX_SOQ_AND_FALLBACK_MODEL
```

### `CM-MKT-RD-012` — Halt·Limit·LULD·Regulatory State Taxonomy

```yaml
record_contract:
  record_id: CM-MKT-RD-012
  source_result_unit_id: CM-MKT-RU-012
  subject: Halt·Limit·LULD·Regulatory State Taxonomy
  original_Result_XYZ_statement: CME Scheduled Halt, CME Price-limit State/Halt, NMS LULD Limit State, LULD Pause, Regulatory Halt, Reopen Auction와
    Remainder-day Termination은 Trigger·Authority·Entry·Exit가 다른 States다. Limit State에서 Trading이 경계가격으로 계속될 수 있으므로 Limit State는 Halt가 아니다. Cash
    LULD를 Futures Halt로 이식하지 않는다.
  applied_Function_2_corrections:
    XY: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    XZ: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
    YZ: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
  preserved_Function_2_positions:
    XY:
      correction_id: XY-MKT-F2-CORR-012
      review_verdict: CORRECT_SOURCE_BINDING
      corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the taxonomy but split source binding by market and authority. CME limit/halt claims require CME sources;
        NMS LULD limit/pause/reopen claims require the current LULD Plan and venue rule; regulatory halt claims require regulator scope. Y-EVT-03
        cannot carry SEC/CME scope from one SEC future-guidance source.
      corrected_classification: PARTIALLY_SOURCE_BACKED_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_TEXT_NEXT_DATA
    XZ:
      correction_id: XZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
      review_verdict: ACCEPT_WITH_BOUNDARY_NOTE
      corrected_statement_or_field: Retain the typed halt/limit taxonomy. Require market, venue, authority, trigger, state_entry, execution_permission,
        state_exit, reopen mechanism and effective rule version for every record. Retain the Function.1 correction that NYSE LULD is a cash-equity
        LULD state, not FUTURES_HALT_STATE.
      corrected_classification: SOURCE_BACKED_STATE_TAXONOMY_WITH_CURRENT_RULE_NEXT_DATA
    YZ:
      correction_id: YZ-MKT-F2-CORR-012
      review_verdict: CORRECT_FORMAL_FIELD
      corrected_statement_or_field: 'Split CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and venue reopen auctions
        by market, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Correct Y-EVT-03''s combined SEC/CME organization scope: its bound SEC
        future guidance cannot source CME mechanics. Current LULD and venue reopen rule text remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.'
      corrected_classification: PARTIAL_SOURCE_BACKING_WITH_MARKET_AUTHORITY_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SPLIT
  canonical_statement: CME scheduled/price-limit states, NMS LULD limit/pause states, regulatory halts and venue reopen auctions are split by
    market, venue, authority, trigger, entry/exit and effective source. Limit State is not automatically Halt; cash LULD is not futures halt.
    CME mechanics require CME sources, LULD requires current Plan/venue rules, and one SEC future-guidance source cannot authorize CME mechanics.
    Current LULD and reopen rule text remain NEXT_DATA_REQUIRED.
  canonical_classification: PARTIAL_SOURCE_BACKING_WITH_MARKET_AUTHORITY_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE_SPLIT
  source_backed_clauses:
  - Limit state is not trading halt; cash LULD and futures halt are different state families.

  schema_candidate_clauses: []

  - X:NYSE-04

  - SRC-G010

  - NYSE-04
  - SEC-04
  - Z:S09
  - NAS/NYSE/CME/SEC exception sources
  - Source Equivalence and Independence Map
  - Bound Y source IDs and Z source refs
  source_independence_state: DEDUPLICATED_BY_CANONICAL_DOCUMENT_VERSION_AND_EFFECTIVE_STATE
  source_authority_scope: SOURCE_AND_QUESTION_SCOPED
  publication_effective_event_retrieval_state:
  - TRIGGER_AND_END_CONDITION_DEPENDENT
  - trigger- and rule-specific
  - branch-specific historical/current scope
  - SEC halt guidance future/current split; CME/venue states current
  - Reopen guidance future-effective; Cross Failure historical; Auction Extension missing
  - Mix of current rules, historical event and methodology states
  market_venue_instrument_scope:
    market: FUTURES_OR_NMS_CASH
    venue: CME_OR_NMS_VENUE
    instrument: null
    contract_or_security: AS_DECLARED
    session: AS_DECLARED
    timezone: AS_DECLARED
  formal_fields:
    market: FUTURES_OR_NMS_CASH
    venue: CME_OR_NMS_VENUE
    state_entry_trigger: REQUIRED
    state_exit_trigger: REQUIRED
    official_authority: CME_RULE_OR_LULD_PLAN_OR_REGULATOR_OR_VENUE
    execution_occurred: STATE_DEPENDENT
    failure_or_fallback: REOPEN_OR_TERMINATION_TYPED
    source_effective_state: CURRENT_TEXT_REQUIRED
  compatible_differences:
  - limit boundary with possible trading
  - temporary suspension
  - regulatory halt
  - auction reopening
  - remainder-day termination
  - futures vs cash-equity vs regulatory
  - operational exception mechanisms
  - order-entry pre-open
  - symbol delayed auction
  - no cross/no auction
  - regulatory suspension
  - price-limit boundary
  - post-halt auction
  - technical cross contingency
  - auction-time extension
  - order modification cutoff
  - normal path
  - fallback
  - failure
  - delay
  - no match
  - halt
  reference_frame_differences: []
  conflicts:
  - Y-EVT-03 combines SEC/CME scope while its bound source is SEC future guidance.
  - X LULD state label uses FUTURES_HALT_STATE
  - Z six transitions do not cover all branches
  - Y-EVT-03 and Y-SES-05 organization scopes exceed their single bound sources.
  - Historical Nasdaq alert cannot define current cross-failure architecture.
  - Official rule defines mechanisms, not every realized outcome.
  ambiguities:
  - HALT_WITHOUT_TRIGGER_AND_END_STATE
  - halt
  - failure
  - Limit state may allow trading to continue and is not necessarily a halt.
  - Cancellation cutoff is a guard/time boundary, not necessarily a market state.
  - Sequence order is not causation and scheduled event may not occur.
  counterexamples:
  - A futures contract can be at a limit while trading continues; an LULD pause can extend in five-minute segments.
  - extended reopening and day termination
  - all eleven X counterexamples
  - NYSE opening auctions failed for thousands of securities while later trading consequences occurred.
  - Validation failure may cancel opening-cross orders instead of generating an official price.
  - NYSE 2023 auction failure; Nasdaq validation cancellation; no-match with indicative price.
  exclusions:
  - Do not label every limit state a halt or every halt a closure.
  - halt as market closed
  - exception as noise
  - Do not encode a single HALT_OR_DELAY enum without authority and mechanism subtype.
  - Do not model Auction Extension as source-backed until a current rule is bound.
  - Do not force every symbol through one deterministic state machine.
  missing_counterparts: []
  unresolved:
  - current LULD Plan exact text
  - current NYSE reopen rule
  - event tape
  - dedicated transitions
  - Current LULD Plan and exact venue halt/reopen rules.
  - Current Nasdaq BCP/DR, current reopen rules and canonical extension rule.
  - Current incident and consolidated event datasets.
  next_data_required:
  - current LULD Plan
  - current NYSE Rule 7.35/reopen text
  - typed halt/reopen transition registry
  - append missing failure branches
  - LULD text, exchange rules and state-entry/exit event records
  - current BCP/DR, venue reopen/extension rules and ordered guard transitions
  - venue incident logs and timestamped symbol-level state traces
  validation_boundary: Current exact Function.1 objects and their recorded source/authority/effective-state boundaries only.
```

```yaml
    Price Prediction을 생성하지 않는다.

        Reclassify 'comparable is not equal/causal/predictive/arbitrage/recommendation' as an internal no-inference boundary, not a source-backed
        market relation.
```

```yaml
  - 09:25 current page versus FAQ footnote describing prior/pending state.

  - Scheduled time does not guarantee actual symbol event time.

  - One Word ≠ One Universal State.
  - Equal missing counts do not imply item identity.

  - REPORT_TIME_AS_OCCURRENCE_TIME

  - failure

  - '`Official` is authority-scoped, not universal factual truth.'

  - Sequence order is not causation and scheduled event may not occur.
  counterexamples:

  - Opening Price can mean component official open, venue official price or index opening value.
  - A formal O11 object does not make a provider methodology available.
  - NYSE 2023 auction failure; Nasdaq validation cancellation; no-match with indicative price.
  exclusions:
  - Do not average authority classes or treat same institution as same document.
  - Do not date the event by the publication date.
  - Do not let FAQ override Rule 4752 or current operating page.
  - Do not delete X:CME-04 or call it nonofficial.

  - Do not treat same canonical source repeated in Y and Z as independent corroboration.

  - Do not fill any missing item by inference.
  - Do not force every symbol through one deterministic state machine.

  - Fifteen semantic missing groups remain.
  - Current incident and consolidated event datasets.
  next_data_required:

  - CME dated change notice or versioned product settlement procedure
```

```yaml
  term_or_axis: First Trade
  ambiguity: Requires venue, eligibility, condition code and event boundary.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-006
  term_or_axis: Executable
  ambiguity: May mean actionable/eligible or execution occurred; fields must split.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-007
  term_or_axis: Final
  ambiguity: May mean event, auction, publication, trade-date, daily settlement or expiration finality.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-008
  term_or_axis: Pre-open
  ambiguity: Order-entry, halt-related or vendor display state.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-009
  term_or_axis: No Open
  ambiguity: No cross, no auction, delayed open or unopened index component.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-010
  term_or_axis: ET/CT
  ambiguity: Requires date-bound DST normalization.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-011
  term_or_axis: Session/Business Date
  ambiguity: No one cross-institution canonical definition in current inputs.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-012
  term_or_axis: Settlement Price
  ambiguity: Daily, preliminary, final daily or expiration settlement.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-013
  term_or_axis: Imbalance
  ambiguity: Quantity/state, not directional prediction or executed volume.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-014
  term_or_axis: Auction Extension
  ambiguity: Current exact rule and scope unresolved.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-015
  term_or_axis: First Firm Quote
  ambiguity: Future-effective benchmark and source-binding issue.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-016
  term_or_axis: Source Identity
  ambiguity: Local IDs may collide or alias the same canonical document.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-017
  term_or_axis: Same Numeric Value
  ambiguity: May belong to different price object and formation mechanism.
- id: CM-MKT-AMB-018
  term_or_axis: Halt/Limit
  ambiguity: Limit state may continue trading; pause/halt/termination differ.
```
## 15. Counterexamples

```yaml
counterexample_count: 13
promoted_to_current_default: 0
counterexamples:
- id: CM-MKT-CE-001
  family: Nasdaq Validation No-cross
  counterexample: Cross validation can produce no auction execution.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-002
  family: Complete Nasdaq Cross-process Failure
  counterexample: Failure may transition/cancel rather than produce an official auction price.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-003
  family: Non-IPO Cancel/Resubmit
  counterexample: Order lifecycle branch contradicts deterministic execution.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-004
  family: NYSE Security-specific Delayed Open
  counterexample: A symbol can open after 09:30 ET while RTH has begun.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-005
  family: NYSE 2023 Auction-not-run Incident
  counterexample: Historical incident demonstrates failure branch, not current default.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-006
  family: Facebook IPO Stale-order-state Failure
  counterexample: Stale order state can invalidate deterministic auction sequence.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-007
  family: SOQ Component Never Opens
  counterexample: Methodology may use last sale or fallback.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-008
  family: Alternative Settlement Input
  counterexample: Settlement may use fallback/discretionary method.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-009
  family: Conditional CME Pre-open Halt
  counterexample: Futures execution can be interrupted despite normal session.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-010
  family: Extended LULD Reopening
  counterexample: Reopen auction/extension is distinct from normal open.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-011
  family: Preliminary Settlement Revision
  counterexample: Published preliminary value may later revise.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-012
  family: Indicative Price Never Executes
  counterexample: Indicative value can change or coexist with zero match.
  current_default: false
- id: CM-MKT-CE-013
  family: Limit State Without Halt
  counterexample: Trading may continue at the limit boundary.
  current_default: false
```

```yaml
    - source equivalence map
    duplicate_source_independence_policy: Same canonical source is counted once unless independent origin is proven.
    source_authority_classes:
    - CONTROLLING_RULE_OR_FINAL_RULE
    - OPERATING_PAGE_OR_SYSTEM_SETTING
    - FAQ_OR_GLOSSARY_OR_RESEARCH_EXPLANATION
    - HISTORICAL_ALERT_OR_ENFORCEMENT_RECORD
    - FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_GUIDANCE
    source_effective_states:
    - CURRENT
    - FUTURE_EFFECTIVE
    - HISTORICAL
    - REVISION_UNKNOWN
    - MISSING_COUNTERPART
    new_web_source_added: false
  mandatory_source_corrections:
  - Canonical source identity is document and version scoped, not local ID scoped.
  - The same canonical source is not independent corroboration.
  - Rule 605 first-firm quote is FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_2026-08-01 at RETRIEVAL_2026-07-25.
  - T04/P05 must bind to recorded Rule 605 sources or retain MISSING_FORMAL_SOURCE_BINDING.
  - Nasdaq historical cross-failure evidence is not current BCP/DR proof.
  - MES official clock versions remain parallel until revision lineage exists.
  - Unsupported institution halves are excluded from source-backed status.
  - Y-EVT-03 SEC source cannot authorize CME mechanics.
  - Derived provider source remains missing.
  effective_state_anchor:
    retrieval_date: '2026-07-25'
    Rule_605_first_firm_quote: FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_2026-08-01
    historical_records: HISTORICAL_OR_HISTORICAL_WITH_LATER_AMENDMENTS
    current_unknown_pages: REVISION_OR_CURRENT_STATUS_UNKNOWN_WHEN_NOT_BOUND
  source_conflicts:
  - MES daily-settlement official clock versions
  - NOII current page versus transition marker
  - Rule 605 current versus future-effective state
  - historical contingency versus current default
  - duplicate source as false corroboration
  - derived provider source absence
```

## 23. Final Guard

```text
Function.2 Integration is not Majority Vote.
Peer Difference is not Permission to Delete.
Source-backed Observation is not Generic Source-backed Rule.
Source-backed Example is not Automatically Source-backed Schema.
Internal Inventory is not External Source.
Future-effective is not Current-effective.
Historical Failure is not Current Default.
Quote is not Trade.
Actionable is not Execution Occurred.
Indicative is not Execution.
Imbalance is not Executed Volume.
Same Numeric Value is not Same Price Object.
Last Trade is not Daily Settlement.
Daily Settlement is not Final Settlement.
Opening Index is not SOQ.
Limit State is not Halt.
Cash LULD is not Futures Halt.
Comparable is not Equal, Causal, Predictive, Arbitrage or Recommendation.
Missing Counterpart is not Confirmed Relation.
Conflict is not Failure to Delete.
Unresolved is not Permission to Infer.
Result.Data is not Track DB.
Relation is not Merge.
```
